
ROM_change_sides = {
	# Allies

	ROM_change_sides_to_allies = {

		icon = decision_generic_nationalism

		allowed = {
			original_tag = ROM
		}

		available = {
			is_faction_leader = no
			has_capitulated = no
			OR = {
				has_country_leader = {
					character = ROM_king_michael
					ruling_only = yes
				}
				has_government = neutrality
				has_government = democratic
			}
			hidden_trigger = {
				has_war = yes
				NOT = { has_country_flag = romania_has_flipped_sides } #Romania shouldn't be able to keep switching sides all the time
			}
			any_enemy_country = {
				is_faction_leader = yes
				OR = {
					has_government = democratic
					original_tag = ENG
					original_tag = USA
				}
			}
			NOT = {
				FACTION_LEADER  = {
					has_government = democratic
				}
			}
		}
		
		visible = {
			is_faction_leader = no # you shouldn't be able to betray your own faction
			original_tag = ROM
			NOT = { has_country_flag = romania_has_flipped_sides }
			hidden_trigger = { has_country_flag = can_change_sides_dem }
		}

		cost = 0
		
		fire_only_once = yes
		
		remove_effect = {
			hidden_effect = { clr_country_flag = can_change_sides_dem }
		}

		complete_effect = {
			#save current faction leader for use in the event
			hidden_effect = {
				random_country = {
					limit = {
						is_in_faction_with = ROOT
						is_faction_leader = yes
					}
					save_global_event_target_as = romania_former_ally_target
				}
			}
			#top priority is joining the Allies if they still exist
			if = {
				limit = {
					any_country = {
						is_faction_leader = yes
						has_war_with = ROM
						has_government = democratic
						OR = {
							original_tag = ENG
							is_in_faction_with = ENG
						}
					}
				}
				random_enemy_country = {
					limit = {
						is_faction_leader = yes
						has_war_with = ROM
						has_government = democratic
						OR = {
							original_tag = ENG
							is_in_faction_with = ENG
						}
					}
					save_event_target_as = romania_new_ally_target
				}
			}
			#then attempts to join the USA's faction
			else_if = {
				limit = {
					any_country = {
						is_faction_leader = yes
						has_war_with = ROM
						has_government = democratic
						OR = {
							original_tag = USA
							is_in_faction_with = USA
						}
					}
				}
				random_enemy_country = {
					limit = {
						is_faction_leader = yes
						has_war_with = ROM
						has_government = democratic
						OR = {
							original_tag = USA
							is_in_faction_with = USA
						}
					}
					save_event_target_as = romania_new_ally_target
				}
			}
			#then attempts to join any democratic faction
			else = {
				random_enemy_country = {
					limit = {
						is_faction_leader = yes
						has_war_with = ROM
						has_government = democratic
					}
					save_event_target_as = romania_new_ally_target
				}
			}
			event_target:romania_new_ally_target = {
				country_event = { id = DOD_romania.16 }
			}
			hidden_effect = {
				set_country_flag = romania_has_flipped_sides
			}
		}

		ai_will_do = {
			factor = 100
			modifier = {
				is_historical_focus_on = no
				factor = 0
			}
			modifier = {
				is_historical_focus_on = yes
				NOT = { date > 1944.08.08 }
				factor = 0
			}
		}

	}

					save_event_target_as = romania_new_ally_target
				}
			}
			#then attempts to join any communist faction
			else = {
				random_enemy_country = {
					limit = {
						is_faction_leader = yes
						has_war_with = ROM
						has_government = communism
					}
					save_event_target_as = romania_new_ally_target
				}
			}
			event_target:romania_new_ally_target = {
				country_event = { id = DOD_romania.16 }
			}
			hidden_effect = {
				set_country_flag = romania_has_flipped_sides
			}
		}

		ai_will_do = {
			factor = 0
		}

	}
	
	# Axis

	ROM_change_sides_to_axis = {

				is_faction_leader = yes
				OR = {
					has_government = fascism
					original_tag = GER
					original_tag = ITA
				}
			}
			NOT = {
				FACTION_LEADER  = {
					has_government = fascism
				}
			}
		}
		
		visible = {
			is_faction_leader = no # you shouldn't be able to betray your own faction
			original_tag = ROM
			NOT = { has_country_flag = romania_has_flipped_sides }
			hidden_trigger = { has_country_flag = can_change_sides_fas }
		}

		cost = 0
		
		fire_only_once = yes
		
		remove_effect = {
			hidden_effect = { clr_country_flag = can_change_sides_fas }
		}

		complete_effect = {

					limit = {
						is_in_faction_with = ROOT

					}

						is_faction_leader = yes
						has_war_with = ROM
						has_government = fascism
						OR = {
							original_tag = GER
							is_in_faction_with = GER
						}
					}
				}
				random_enemy_country = {
					limit = {
						is_faction_leader = yes
						has_war_with = ROM
						has_government = fascism
						OR = {
							original_tag = GER
							is_in_faction_with = GER
						}
					}
					save_event_target_as = romania_new_ally_target
				}
			}
			#then attempts to join Italy's faction
			else_if = {
				limit = {
					any_country = {
						is_faction_leader = yes
						has_war_with = ROM
						has_government = fascism
						OR = {
							original_tag = ITA
							is_in_faction_with = ITA
						}
					}
				}
				random_enemy_country = {
					limit = {
						is_faction_leader = yes
						has_war_with = ROM
						has_government = fascism
						OR = {
							original_tag = ITA
							is_in_faction_with = ITA
						}
					}
					save_event_target_as = romania_new_ally_target
				}
			}
			#then attempts to join any fascist faction
			else = {
				random_enemy_country = {
					limit = {
						is_faction_leader = yes
						has_war_with = ROM
						has_government = fascism
					}
					save_event_target_as = romania_new_ally_target

ROM_dividing_yugo = {

	# Pressing claims

	ROM_present_ultimatum = {

		icon = decision_generic_nationalism

		allowed = {
			original_tag = ROM
		}

		available = {
			YUG = {
				NOT = {
					is_ally_with = ROOT
				}
				all_subject_countries = {
					tooltip = ROM_country_and_all_subjects_trigger
					all_owned_state = {
						OR = {
							has_state_flag = strongest_claimant_ROM
							has_state_flag = strongest_claimant_HUN
							has_state_flag = strongest_claimant_GER
							has_state_flag = strongest_claimant_ITA
							AND = {
								is_claimed_by = GER
								GER = { has_country_flag = invited_to_split_yugo }
							}
							AND = {
								is_claimed_by = ITA
								ITA = { has_country_flag = invited_to_split_yugo }
							}
							AND = {
								is_claimed_by = HUN
								HUN = { has_country_flag = invited_to_split_yugo }
							}
							is_claimed_by = ROM
						}
					}
				}
				# This trigger is the same as the one for the subject countries above,
				# so the tooltip will be correct with an overridden trigger start for the
				# all_Subject_countries
				hidden_trigger = {
					all_owned_state = {
						OR = {
							has_state_flag = strongest_claimant_ROM
							has_state_flag = strongest_claimant_HUN
							has_state_flag = strongest_claimant_GER
							has_state_flag = strongest_claimant_ITA
							AND = {
								is_claimed_by = GER
								GER = { has_country_flag = invited_to_split_yugo }
							}
							AND = {
								is_claimed_by = ITA
								ITA = { has_country_flag = invited_to_split_yugo }
							}
							AND = {
								is_claimed_by = HUN
								HUN = { has_country_flag = invited_to_split_yugo }
							}
							is_claimed_by = ROM
						}
					}
				}
			}
		}
		
		visible = {
		}

		cost = 0
		
		fire_only_once = yes

		complete_effect = {
			YUG = {
				country_event = DOD_romania.60
			}
		}

		ai_will_do = {
			factor = 100
		}

	}

	# Invite Germany

	ROM_invite_germany = {

		icon = decision_generic_nationalism

		allowed = {
			original_tag = ROM
		}

		available = {
			GER = {
				NOT = { has_country_flag = invited_to_split_yugo }
				NOT = { has_war_with = ROOT }
				any_owned_state = {
					any_neighbor_state = {
						owner = {
							OR = {
								tag = YUG
								is_subject_of = YUG
							}
						}
					}
				}
			}
		}
		
		visible = {
		}

		cost = 0
		
		fire_only_once = yes

		complete_effect = {

	# Claiming Vojvodina

	ROM_claim_vojvodina = {

		#ROM
		icon = {
			key = GFX_decision_ROM_claim_yugo_4
			trigger = {
				1514 = { # eu_mapping: v45 (Vojvodina) -> ME 1514 (Novi Sad)
					1514 = { check_variable = {  var = claim_strength_ROM value = 3 compare = greater_than_or_equals } } # eu_mapping: v45 (Vojvodina) -> ME 1514 (Novi Sad)
					has_state_flag = strongest_claimant_ROM
				}
			}
		}
		icon = {
			key = GFX_decision_ROM_claim_yugo_3
			trigger = {
				1514 = { # eu_mapping: v45 (Vojvodina) -> ME 1514 (Novi Sad)
					1514 = { check_variable = {  var = claim_strength_ROM value = 2 compare = greater_than_or_equals } } # eu_mapping: v45 (Vojvodina) -> ME 1514 (Novi Sad)
					has_state_flag = strongest_claimant_ROM
				}
			}
		}
		icon = {
			key = GFX_decision_ROM_claim_yugo_2
			trigger = {
				1514 = { # eu_mapping: v45 (Vojvodina) -> ME 1514 (Novi Sad)
					1514 = { check_variable = {  var = claim_strength_ROM value = 1 compare = greater_than_or_equals } } # eu_mapping: v45 (Vojvodina) -> ME 1514 (Novi Sad)
					has_state_flag = strongest_claimant_ROM
				}
			}
		}
		icon = {
			key = GFX_decision_ROM_claim_yugo_1
			trigger = {
				1514 = { # eu_mapping: v45 (Vojvodina) -> ME 1514 (Novi Sad)
					NOT = {
						has_state_flag = strongest_claimant_GER
						has_state_flag = strongest_claimant_ROM
						has_state_flag = strongest_claimant_ITA
						has_state_flag = strongest_claimant_HUN
					}
				}
			}
		}

		#ITA
		icon = {
			key = GFX_decision_ITA_claim_yugo_4

					has_state_flag = strongest_claimant_ITA
				}
			}
		}
		icon = {
			key = GFX_decision_ITA_claim_yugo_2
			trigger = {
				1514 = { # eu_mapping: v45 (Vojvodina) -> ME 1514 (Novi Sad)
					1514 = { check_variable = {  var = claim_strength_ITA value = 1 compare = greater_than_or_equals } } # eu_mapping: v45 (Vojvodina) -> ME 1514 (Novi Sad)
					has_state_flag = strongest_claimant_ITA
				}
			}

		icon = {
			key = GFX_decision_HUN_claim_yugo_4
			trigger = {
				1514 = { # eu_mapping: v45 (Vojvodina) -> ME 1514 (Novi Sad)
					1514 = { check_variable = {  var = claim_strength_HUN value = 3 compare = greater_than_or_equals } } # eu_mapping: v45 (Vojvodina) -> ME 1514 (Novi Sad)

			trigger = {

					1514 = { check_variable = {  var = claim_strength_HUN value = 2 compare = greater_than_or_equals } } # eu_mapping: v45 (Vojvodina) -> ME 1514 (Novi Sad)
					has_state_flag = strongest_claimant_HUN

			trigger = {

		#GER
		icon = {

			trigger = {

					has_state_flag = strongest_claimant_GER
				}
			}
		}
		icon = {
			key = GFX_decision_GER_claim_yugo_3
			trigger = {
				1514 = { # eu_mapping: v45 (Vojvodina) -> ME 1514 (Novi Sad)
					1514 = { check_variable = {  var = claim_strength_GER value = 2 compare = greater_than_or_equals } } # eu_mapping: v45 (Vojvodina) -> ME 1514 (Novi Sad)
					has_state_flag = strongest_claimant_GER
				}
			}
		}
		icon = {
			key = GFX_decision_GER_claim_yugo_2
			trigger = {
				1514 = { # eu_mapping: v45 (Vojvodina) -> ME 1514 (Novi Sad)
					1514 = { check_variable = {  var = claim_strength_GER value = 1 compare = greater_than_or_equals } } # eu_mapping: v45 (Vojvodina) -> ME 1514 (Novi Sad)
					has_state_flag = strongest_claimant_GER
				}
			}
		}

		allowed = {
		}

		available = {
			1514 = { # eu_mapping: v45 (Vojvodina) -> ME 1514 (Novi Sad)
				has_anyone_else_claimed_ROM = yes
			}
			OR = {
				GER = {
					has_country_flag = invited_to_split_yugo
				}
				ITA = {
					has_country_flag = invited_to_split_yugo
				}
				HUN = {
					has_country_flag = invited_to_split_yugo
				}
			}
		}
		
		visible = {
			has_country_flag = invited_to_split_yugo
			hidden_trigger = {
				NOT = { has_country_flag = claiming_a_yugoslav_state_flag }
			}
			1514 = { # eu_mapping: v45 (Vojvodina) -> ME 1514 (Novi Sad)
				owner = {
					OR = {
						tag = YUG
						is_subject_of = YUG
					}
				}
			}
		}
		
		highlight_states = {
			highlight_states_trigger = {
				OR = {
					state = 1514 # eu_mapping: v45 (Vojvodina) -> ME 1514 (Novi Sad)
					state = 735 # eu_mapping: v45 (Vojvodina) -> ME 735 (Subotica)
					state = 1472 # eu_mapping: v45 (Vojvodina) -> ME 1472 (Baranja)
					state = 1515 # eu_mapping: v45 (Vojvodina) -> ME 1515 (Sombor)
					state = 1516 # eu_mapping: v45 (Vojvodina) -> ME 1516 (Senta)
					state = 2856 # eu_mapping: v45 (Vojvodina) -> ME 2856 (Bezdan)
				}
			}
		}

		on_map_mode = map_only

		cost = 20
		
		fire_only_once = no
		days_remove = 7
		
		remove_effect = {
			1514 = { # eu_mapping: v45 (Vojvodina) -> ME 1514 (Novi Sad)
				custom_effect_tooltip = romania_total_claims_tt
				calculate_claimant_effect = yes
			}
			hidden_effect = {
				735 = { calculate_claimant_effect = yes } # eu_mapping: v45 (Vojvodina) -> ME 735 (Subotica)

				}
			}

	ROM_sell_current_harvest = {
			icon = GFX_decision_generic_trade
			target_array = global.rom_diplomacy_partners
			visible = {
				has_variable = ROM_diplo_selected_country
				var:ROM_diplo_selected_country = {
					tag = FROM
				}
				OR = {
					has_idea = ROM_harvest_excellent
					has_idea = ROM_harvest_good
					has_idea = ROM_harvest_bad
					has_idea = ROM_harvest_terrible
				}
				FROM = {
					NOT = { has_country_flag = ROM_accepted_harvest_trade }
				}				
			}
			available = {
				has_idea = free_trade
				NOT = { has_country_flag = ROM_proposing_harvest_trade }
				custom_trigger_tooltip = {
					tooltip = {
						localization_key = ROM_has_relation_target_higher
						TARGET = FROM
						AMOUNT = 3
					}
					FROM = {
						check_variable = { ROM_relation_var > 2.5 }
					}
				}
				custom_trigger_tooltip = {
					tooltip = ROM_recently_refused_harvest_tt
					FROM = {
						NOT = { has_country_flag = ROM_refused_harvest_trade }
					}
				}
				custom_trigger_tooltip = {
					tooltip = ROM_accepted_harvest_tt
					FROM = {
						NOT = { has_country_flag = ROM_accepted_harvest_trade }
					}
				}								
			}
			
			cost = 75
			fire_only_once = no #it actually fires only once if they accept. 

			complete_effect = {
				set_country_flag = ROM_proposing_harvest_trade
				
				if = { limit = { has_idea = ROM_harvest_excellent } set_country_flag = ROM_selling_excellent }
				else_if = { limit = { has_idea = ROM_harvest_good } set_country_flag = ROM_selling_good }
				else_if = { limit = { has_idea = ROM_harvest_bad } set_country_flag = ROM_selling_bad }
				else_if = { limit = { has_idea = ROM_harvest_terrible } set_country_flag = ROM_selling_terrible }

				FROM = {
					country_event = { id = BA_romania_harvest_trade.1 hours = 6 }
				}

				custom_effect_tooltip = if_they_accept_newline_tt
				effect_tooltip = {
					if = {
						limit = { has_idea = ROM_harvest_excellent }
						remove_ideas = ROM_harvest_excellent
						custom_effect_tooltip = generic_skip_one_line_tt
						add_political_power = 30
						add_stability = -0.02
					}
					else_if = {
						limit = { has_idea = ROM_harvest_good }
						remove_ideas = ROM_harvest_good
						custom_effect_tooltip = generic_skip_one_line_tt
						add_political_power = 20
						add_stability = -0.05
					}
					else_if = {
						limit = { has_idea = ROM_harvest_bad }
						remove_ideas = ROM_harvest_bad
						custom_effect_tooltip = generic_skip_one_line_tt
						add_political_power = 10
						add_stability = -0.10
						add_war_support = -0.05
					}
					else_if = {
						limit = { has_idea = ROM_harvest_terrible }
						remove_ideas = ROM_harvest_terrible
						custom_effect_tooltip = generic_skip_one_line_tt
						add_stability = -0.10
						add_war_support = -0.10
					}
					
					custom_effect_tooltip = generic_skip_one_line_tt

					FROM = {
						add_timed_idea = { idea = ROM_imported_romanian_harvest days = 120 }
					}

					custom_effect_tooltip = generic_skip_one_line_tt

					if = {
						limit = { FROM = { OR = { original_tag = GER original_tag = ITA } } }
						add_offsite_building = { type = arms_factory level = 2 }
						add_command_power = 25
						army_experience = 40
					}
					else_if = {
						limit = { FROM = { OR = { original_tag = ENG original_tag = FRA } } }
						add_offsite_building = { type = industrial_complex level = 1 }
						add_political_power = 25
					}
					else_if = {
						limit = { FROM = { original_tag = SOV original_tag = ITA } }
						add_equipment_to_stockpile = { type = infantry_equipment amount = 3000 producer = FROM }
						add_equipment_to_stockpile = { type = artillery_equipment amount = 250 producer = FROM }
					}
					else_if = {
						limit = { FROM = { OR = { original_tag = CZE original_tag = POL } } }
						add_offsite_building = { type = arms_factory level = 1 }
						add_equipment_to_stockpile = { type = support_equipment amount = 750 producer = FROM }
					}
					else = {
						add_offsite_building = { type = arms_factory level = 1 }
						add_offsite_building = { type = industrial_complex level = 1 }
					}
				}
			}
		}

			custom_trigger_tooltip = {
				tooltip = {
					localization_key = ROM_has_relation_target_higher
					TARGET = FROM
					AMOUNT = 2
				}
				FROM = {
					check_variable = { ROM_relation_var > 1.5 }
				}
			}
			if = {
				limit = {
					FROM = {
						original_tag = CZE
					}
				}
				NOT = {
					has_idea = CZE_trade_agreement
				}
			}
		}
		remove_effect = {
			FROM = {	#reverse trade idea with meta effect?
				add_ideas = ROM_trade_agreement
			}
			if = {
				limit = {
					has_dlc = "Arms Against Tyranny"
				}
				if = {
					limit = {
						FROM = {
							OR = {
								original_tag = GER
								original_tag = FRA
								original_tag = POL
							}
						}
					}
					custom_effect_tooltip = generic_skip_one_line_tt
					add_equipment_subsidy = {
						cic = 100
						equipment_type = small_plane_airframe
						seller_tags = { FROM }
					}
				}
				else_if = {
					limit = {
						FROM = {
							original_tag = CZE
						}
					}
					custom_effect_tooltip = generic_skip_one_line_tt
					add_equipment_subsidy = {
						cic = 100
						equipment_type = infantry_equipment
						seller_tags = { CZE }
					}
				}
				if = {
					limit = {
						FROM = {
							OR = {
								original_tag = FRA
								original_tag = CZE
							}
						}
					}
					add_equipment_subsidy = {
						cic = 100
						equipment_type = light_tank_chassis
						seller_tags = { FROM }
					}
					custom_effect_tooltip = generic_skip_one_line_tt
				}
				else_if = {
					limit = {
						FROM = {
							original_tag = GER
							OR = {
								NOT = {
									has_dlc = "No Step Back"
								}
								has_design_based_on = medium_tank_chassis
							}
						}
					}
					add_equipment_subsidy = {
						cic = 100
						equipment_type = medium_tank_chassis
						seller_tags = { GER }
					}
					custom_effect_tooltip = generic_skip_one_line_tt
				}
				else_if = {
					limit = {
						FROM = {
							OR = {
								original_tag = GER
								original_tag = POL
							}
						}
					}
					custom_effect_tooltip = generic_skip_one_line_tt
				}
			}
			add_opinion_modifier = {
				target = FROM
				modifier = generic_small_trade_agreement
			}
			reverse_add_opinion_modifier = {
				target = FROM
				modifier = generic_small_trade_agreement
			}
		}
	}
	ROM_purchase_tanks_to_analyze = {
		icon = GFX_decision_generic_tank
		target_array = global.rom_diplomacy_partners
		target_root_trigger = {
			has_completed_focus = ROM_study_foreign_tank_designs
		}
		target_trigger = {
			FROM = {
				OR = {
					original_tag = FRA
					original_tag = POL
					original_tag = SOV
					original_tag = CZE
				}
			}
		}
		visible = {
			has_variable = ROM_diplo_selected_country
			var:ROM_diplo_selected_country = {
				tag = FROM
			}
		}
		custom_cost_trigger = {
			num_of_civilian_factories_available_for_projects > 1
		}
		custom_cost_text = decision_cost_civ_factory_2
		available = {
			custom_trigger_tooltip = {
				tooltip = {
					localization_key = ROM_has_relation_target_higher
					TARGET = FROM
					AMOUNT = 2
				}
				FROM = {
					check_variable = { ROM_relation_var > 1.5 }
				}
			}
		}
		fire_only_once = yes
		days_remove = 30
		modifier = {
			civilian_factory_use = 2
		}
		remove_effect = {
			FROM = {
				set_country_flag = ROM_bought_our_tanks_flag
			}
			if = {
				limit = {
					FROM = {
						original_tag = FRA
					}
				}
				add_equipment_to_stockpile = {
					type = heavy_tank_chassis	#automatically gives newest, otherwise would be _1
					amount = 40
					producer = FROM
				}
				add_tech_bonus = {
					name = ROM_study_foreign_tank_designs
					bonus = 0.5
					uses = 1
					category = cat_heavy_armor
				}
				FROM = {
					set_country_flag = ROM_trialed_our_heavy_tanks
				}
			}
			else_if = {
				limit = {	#T-34 develop for example? Give them that!
					FROM = {
						OR = {
							AND = {
								has_tech = basic_medium_tank_chassis
								has_design_based_on = basic_medium_tank_chassis
							}
							has_tech = basic_medium_tank
							AND = {
								original_tag = SOV
								ROOT = {
									has_country_flag = ROM_has_studied_light_tanks
								}
							}
						}
					}
				}
				add_equipment_to_stockpile = {
					type = medium_tank_chassis
					amount = 50
					producer = FROM
				}
				add_tech_bonus = {
					name = ROM_study_foreign_tank_designs
					bonus = 0.5
					uses = 1
					category = cat_medium_armor
				}
				FROM = {
					set_country_flag = ROM_trialed_our_medium_tanks
				}
			}
			else = {
				# limit = {
				# 	FROM = {
				# 		OR = {
				# 			original_tag = CZE
				# 			original_tag = POL
				# 			original_tag = SOV
				# 		}
				# 	}
				# }
				add_equipment_to_stockpile = {
					type = light_tank_chassis #automatically gives newest, otherwise would be _2 from CZE
					amount = 60
					producer = FROM
				}
				add_tech_bonus = {
					name = ROM_study_foreign_tank_designs
					bonus = 0.5
					uses = 1
					category = cat_light_armor
				}
				set_country_flag = ROM_has_studied_light_tanks
				FROM = {
					set_country_flag = ROM_trialed_our_light_tanks
				}
			}
			custom_effect_tooltip = generic_skip_one_line_tt
			custom_effect_tooltip = ROM_unlock_diplo_cat_decisions_tt
			activate_targeted_decision = {
				target = FROM
				decision = ROM_set_up_production_of_FROM_tanks
			}
		}
	}
	ROM_set_up_production_of_FROM_tanks = {
		icon = GFX_decision_generic_merge_plant_tank
		target_array = global.rom_diplomacy_partners
		target_root_trigger = {
			has_completed_focus = ROM_study_foreign_tank_designs
		}
		target_trigger = {
			FROM = {
				OR = {
					original_tag = FRA
					original_tag = POL
					original_tag = SOV
					original_tag = CZE
				}
			}
		}
		visible = {
			has_variable = ROM_diplo_selected_country
			var:ROM_diplo_selected_country = {
				tag = FROM
			}
			FROM = {
				has_country_flag = ROM_bought_our_tanks_flag
			}
		}
		available = {
			custom_trigger_tooltip = {
				tooltip = {
					localization_key = ROM_has_relation_target_higher
					TARGET = FROM
					AMOUNT = 3
				}
				FROM = {
					check_variable = { ROM_relation_var > 2.5 }
				}
			}
		}
		fire_only_once = yes
		days_remove = 60
		remove_effect = {
			if = {
				limit = {
					has_dlc = "No Step Back"
				}
				if = {
					limit = {
						FROM = {
							has_country_flag = ROM_trialed_our_heavy_tanks
							has_design_based_on = heavy_tank_chassis
						}
					}
					if = {
						limit = {
							FROM = {
								has_tech = basic_heavy_tank_chassis
							}
						}
						FROM = {
							create_production_license = {
								target = ROOT
								equipment = {
									type = heavy_tank_chassis_1
									version = 0
								}
								cost_factor = 0
							}
						}
					}
					else = {
						FROM = {
							create_production_license = {
								target = ROOT
								equipment = {
									type = heavy_tank_chassis_0
									version = 0
								}
								cost_factor = 0
							}
						}
					}
				}
				else_if = {
					limit = {
						FROM = {
							has_country_flag = ROM_trialed_our_medium_tanks
							has_design_based_on = medium_tank_chassis
						}
					}
					if = {
						limit = {
							FROM = {
								has_tech = basic_medium_tank_chassis
							}
						}
						FROM = {
							create_production_license = {
								target = ROOT
								equipment = {
									type = medium_tank_chassis_1
									version = 0
								}
								cost_factor = 0
							}
						}
					}
					else = {
						FROM = {
							create_production_license = {
								target = ROOT
								equipment = {
									type = medium_tank_chassis_0
									version = 0
								}
								cost_factor = 0
							}
						}
					}
				}
				else_if = {
					limit = {
						FROM = {
							#has_country_flag = ROM_trialed_our_light_tanks		#this is the backup option so we'll ignore whether lights were actually the ones trialed
							has_design_based_on = light_tank_chassis
						}
					}
					if = {
						limit = {
							FROM = {
								has_tech = improved_light_tank_chassis
								OR = {
									original_tag = CZE
									original_tag = SOV
									#the above start with a design, France for example doesnt and could license invalid designs
									has_equipment = {
										light_tank_chassis_2 > 0
									}
								}
							}
						}
						FROM = {
							create_production_license = {
								target = ROOT
								equipment = {
									type = light_tank_chassis_2
									version = 0
								}
								cost_factor = 0
							}
						}
					}
					else_if = {
						limit = {
							FROM = {
								has_tech = basic_light_tank_chassis
							}
						}
						FROM = {
							create_production_license = {
								target = ROOT
								equipment = {
									type = light_tank_chassis_1
									version = 0
								}
								cost_factor = 0
							}
						}
					}
					else = {
						FROM = {
							create_production_license = {
								target = ROOT
								equipment = {
									type = light_tank_chassis_0
									version = 0
								}
								cost_factor = 0
							}
						}
					}
				}
			}
		}
	}

	ROM_joint_fortification_program = {
		icon = GFX_decision_generic_mountain_fortification
		visible = {
			is_in_faction = yes
			has_completed_focus = ROM_promote_collective_defense_programs
		}
		available = {
			any_allied_country = {				
				is_neighbor_of = ROOT
			}
		}
		fire_only_once = yes
		complete_effect = {
			add_ideas = ROM_joint_fort_program
			custom_effect_tooltip = generic_skip_one_line_tt
			effect_tooltip = {
				every_faction_member = {
					limit = {
						NOT = {
							tag = PREV
						}
					}
					country_event = BA_romania.19
				}
			}
			hidden_effect = {
				every_faction_member = {
					limit = {
						NOT = {
							tag = ROOT
						}
					}
					if = {
						limit = {
							is_neighbor_of = ROOT
							any_neighbor_country = {
								is_neighbor_of = ROOT
							}
						}
						country_event = BA_romania.19
					}
					else = {	#to avoid doing too much recursion on neighbors, just having the further ones fire later
						country_event = {
							id = BA_romania.19
							days = 15
						}
					}
				}
			}
			custom_effect_tooltip = ROM_joint_fortification_program_tt
			custom_effect_tooltip = generic_skip_one_line_tt
		}
		custom_cost_trigger = {
			command_power > 49
			has_political_power > 99
		}
		custom_cost_text = decision_cost_CP_50_PP_100
		days_remove = 240

		remove_effect = {
			hidden_effect = {
				add_political_power = -100
				add_command_power = -50
			}
			every_faction_member = {
				limit = {
					has_country_flag = ROM_fort_program_contributor
				}
				add_to_temp_variable = {
					ROOT.contributed_factories = num_of_factories
				}
			}
			every_faction_member = {
				limit = {
					OR = {
						tag = ROOT
						AND = {
							is_ally_with = ROOT
							has_country_flag = ROM_part_of_fort_program_flag
						}
					}
				}
				every_core_state = {
					limit = {
						is_owned_by = PREV
						any_neighbor_state = {
							owner = {
								NOT = {
									is_ally_with = ROOT
									has_war_with = ROOT	#lets not pop up forts right in front of a frontline
								}
							}
						}
					}
					if = {
						limit = {
							check_variable = {
								ROOT.contributed_factories > 75
							}
						}
						if = {
							limit = {
								infrastructure < 3
							}
							add_building_construction = {
								type = infrastructure
								level = 2
							}
						}
						else_if = {
							limit = {
								infrastructure < 4
							}
							add_building_construction = {
								type = infrastructure
								level = 1
							}
						}
						add_building_construction = {
							type = bunker
							level = 3
							instant_build = yes
							province = {
								limit_to_border = yes
							}
						}
					}
					else = {
						if = {
							limit = {
								infrastructure < 3
							}
							add_building_construction = {
								type = infrastructure
								level = 1
							}
						}
						add_building_construction = {
							type = bunker
							level = 2
							instant_build = yes
							province = {
								limit_to_border = yes
							}
						}
					}
				}
			}
		}
	}

					}
				}
				1579 = { # eu_mapping: v77 (Dobrudja) -> ME 1579 (Durostor)
					add_dynamic_modifier = {
						modifier = ROM_regional_autonomy
					}
				}
			}
		}
	}
}
